Owner [B0232](../02%20Readings/T7S_B0232_DEEP_READING.md), reading SHA256 `bcbeba5a62b8d606c2f9582c1aa15c4ffc5cd04f6f7583c99ff4d85656ea1f07`; source-lock12.140, witness `T7S_GAME_OFFLINE_JA_R484`, corpus `c20260909-r484`. **Current character qualifiers appended by occurrence:** these notes qualify existing CHAR routes; no new CHAR/UNIT/READY, and no later state backdated.

- `CHAR-0004` / Haru (`kasukabe-haru`), `402000401:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-001`: Haru devotes time to an ordinary preference and defends its value before an imagined dismissive response. The message makes attention to her chosen appearance a small relational invitation: a planned encounter tomorrow replaces the proposed photograph. Her account widens the portrait beyond public cheerfulness or crisis care without requiring an idol-performance motive for this purchase. Existing CHAR0004 identifies crisis-tested peer/performer development and retains limits on compulsory certainty, technical mastery and durable resilience. This bounded ordinary preference adds a context without curing those limits or promoting READY0001. Rivals/limits: The question about what the manager is thinking supplies Haru's rhetorical anticipation, not an actual dismissive manager turn. Two hours of selection can show invested attention; it does not establish incompetence, compulsive spending, a general personality rule or a diagnostic condition. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0059` / Kyoko (`uesugi-uebasu-kyoko`), `402000501:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-002`: The message moves from command and insult to an attributed collective concern, then emphatically protects Kyoko from being read as the concerned individual. Her closing demand preserves the wish for return after the denial. This makes the handling of concern analytically visible while leaving her private motive open: defensive personal investment and literal representation of others' concern remain compatible readings. CHAR0059/IDENTITY0077 and READY0027 retain Kyoko's plural performer/actor/person relations and the earlier Ferb exchange's person-specific care. This small address does not settle a hidden true self, general coping rule, romance or readiness promotion. Rivals/limits: Kyoko's explicit denial cannot simply be translated into a confession of romantic love; it is still her asserted distinction between herself and everyone. Reported unanswered calls and late return do not establish deliberate avoidance, danger, guilt or a duty to answer every call immediately. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0015` / Shizuka (`kuonji-shizuka`), `402000601:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-003`: The new communication tool is linked by Shizuka to the outward mobility of idol work. She takes the initiative to use it and supplies a practical second channel for checking whether the first succeeded. Her elaborate courtesy and uncertainty coexist with an executable plan; unfamiliarity with this exchange does not amount to general incapacity or passive dependence. CHAR0015/IDENTITY0018 and READY0008 distinguish Shizuka's sheltered unfamiliarity from discipline, initiative and coordination. The new tool extends the field of outward experience while leaving family permissions, material dependence and later practice unproved; Mana remains a separate subject, CHAR0017. Rivals/limits: The benefactive 買っていただきました reports someone buying the device for her, not an independently financed purchase. The buyer is not named as Mana, her grandfather or another family member. First email here can describe this first attempt/exchange; it need not prove she has never used any email before. The planned phone check is neither an accomplished call nor evidence that delivery actually failed. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0009` / Hime (`nonohara-hime`), `402000701:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-004`: Hime's purchase and her fear of another person's control over its use are separate acts. The benefactive description of peers choosing clothes and the rueful つい do not let the analyst call the entire purchase forced, while her dress-up-doll prediction marks a boundary around discovery and subsequent use. Asking the manager about storage makes that private negotiation concrete without granting the manager or Coney general authority over her appearance. Hime CHAR0009/READY0004 preserve chosen cuteness alongside gruff speech and particular costume refusals. Targeted published B0188 paragraphs and EV-B0188-006/007 were actually retrieved (reading SHA256 58a534a5bbda3974857a66222c6330fc6e3147084e448b737466705f10dcff33): choosing adornment or asking to be seen retains taste limits and supplies no unlimited image authority. Those independent occurrences do not date this shopping trip. Rivals/limits: Frilly clothes and オレ coexist. Buying them need not reveal a single hidden feminine self, cure her embarrassment or invalidate earlier costume refusals. Coney's feared response is Hime's prospective appraisal, not an enacted new coercion or direct access to Coney's plan. Peer assistance likewise is not proof of unanimous agreement or pressure-free selection. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0065` / Mimi (`futagawa-mimi`), `402000801:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-005`: Mimi shares pleasure in another person's presentation and offers to connect her university social world with the manager. The proposed introduction opens a relation while the closing warning marks how she wants that opening to be received. Her older-sister self-address is playful here rather than evidence of literal kinship or standing authority over either person. CHAR0065/IDENTITY0042 preserve B0086's reciprocal older-sister relation and willingness to learn from younger members' unfinishedness. This outward social invitation adds ordinary context without making her a universal expert or promoting readiness. Rivals/limits: The warning may be protective teasing, playful jealousy or a conventional caution. It is no proof of the manager's actual desire, Mimi's exclusive claim or a settled romance. The friend's universal cuteness and the enjoyable selection are Mimi's appraisal. No friend's own preference, consent, reaction or independent image is supplied; enthusiasm alone proves neither successful styling nor coercion. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0060` / Ferb (`seto-ferb`), `402000901:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-006`: Ferb initiates the shopping, supplies a value criterion and participates in a technical conversation before asking for help deciding. Her invitation makes the choice relational without erasing that existing engagement. The conditional if-you-have-time opening and the tentative 欲しいような気もする retain limits that the expectant closing does not convert into an accomplished visit or a general delegation of musical judgment. CHAR0060/IDENTITY0078 and READY0028 retain chosen QOP affiliation, person-specific but fallible interpretation and reciprocal labor with Kyoko. Rivals/limits: コスパの高い concerns favorable cost performance; it is not a statement that the item is expensive or a verified technical evaluation. Wanting the manager involved need not mean Ferb lacks expertise, that the manager has superior equipment knowledge, that the item will be his gift, or that every future artistic choice is delegated. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0012` / Momoka (`serizawa-momoka`), `402001001:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-007`: Momoka turns selective leisure enthusiasm into a proposed collective activity and then into a work-compatible justification. The 'sommelier' reputation and 'exemplary idol' praise are part of her persuasive self-presentation, rather than independent assessments. Her effort toward the desired activity and the attempt to negotiate its legitimacy extend selective engagement without establishing either useful training or actual evasion of work. CHAR0012/IDENTITY0015 and READY0005 retain selective competence, repeated interest-bound work/withdrawal and differentiated consent states. The current message supports persuasion around a desired activity, without reducing Momoka to universal laziness or proving enduring vocational reform. Rivals/limits: Anime may be useful study in some circumstances, but this message gives no title, training goal or learning result that would verify the claim. The voiced 'え？仕事しなきゃ？' is an objection accessed through Momoka's own uptake, not a separately authored manager line or proof of his private evaluation. Approval remains requested rather than received. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0003` / Nicole (`rokusaki-connie-presented`), `402001101:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-008`: A claimed direct email gives fans access to domestic comedy and a reported peer disagreement. Rough plural address and practical care questions make familiarity part of Nicole's public persona; the sudden closing stages a limit to the backstage access just offered. The anecdote is asymmetrical: Nicole supplies both the cooking account and Mito's reaction, while Mito contributes no own turn, reason or consent to having the episode circulated. Mito's increasing quietness is Nicole's generalization, not a verified cross-context psychological rule or an acoustic observation by the analyst. The tofu attempt gives no independent recipe result or explanation of Mito's anger. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0047` / Mito (`hanyuda-mito`), `402001201:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-009`: Mito makes the production brief visible inside the requested friendly address. She tests a possible model and chooses plainness rather than reproducing a style she judges unsuitable. This is agency within a commissioned form, not proof that the text is an unmediated private self. Her account of answering Nico also keeps relation present within sparse emailing; low output alone does not show a lack of attachment or communicative ability. CHAR0047/IDENTITY0060 and READY0019 retain Mito's resistance to prefabricated feeling, exact artistic/domestic attention, attachment and bounded autonomy. The new brief tests a genre of friendly publicity without resolving wider history, later life, relationship privacy or performed voice. Rivals/limits: Nico's messages being terrible or excessively frequent is Mito's appraisal, not an independently inspected sample or a general judgment by the analyst. The text does not settle a single motive, prove incapacity to write warmly or establish complete freedom from the brief. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0017` / Mana (`misonoo-mana`), `402001301:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-010`: Mana uses a formal letter convention to share a personally meaningful family activity. Her wish to circulate an image meets reported household opposition, while her closing question exposes a different mismatch about the communication form itself. These are distinct limits on an attempted address: neither the family's reasons nor the unvoiced interlocutor's exact correction becomes transparently known through her uptake. Targeted published B0194 family/brother/circulation passages were retrieved (SHA256 45fc1da529c941364fc16373ae5ba0cd374f6238681ba871725c1e4d2553d808): particular objections do not decide every question of family freedom. The protective warning does not establish that the manager has already caused tears, that any peer has complained, or that jealousy is her sole hidden motive. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0070` / Miwako (`asami-miwako`), `402004201:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-039`: Teaching is presented as reciprocal motivation rather than one-way adult competence. Miwako acknowledges the cost of her own combined work, admires the members' effort and makes them a standard for herself. Her offer to the manager extends this exchange of support without reporting an actual consultation or resolving anyone's workload. IDENTITY0043/CHAR0070 retain the acting/disguise route and B0094's concern for younger members' education and plural futures, including reopening possibilities for herself. This message supplies a separate paper-and-tutoring occasion, without a chosen new career, exact age or readiness promotion. Rivals/limits: Writing a paper does not establish a specified subject, completed degree, publication, academic appointment or successful assessment. Her praise of the members and the manager's selection is her appraisal, not independently established academic results or infallible management. Enjoyment coexists with the stated difficulty; neither cancels the other or guarantees a sustainable schedule. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0031` / Ayumu (`origasa-ayumu`), `402004301:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-040`: The mail transfers a valued service performance into the correspondence when Nanasta work restricts her cafe visits. Cute phonetic play, honorific address and typed decorations make the message itself a small performance rather than merely reporting a wish. The promise of exclusivity is hers within that register; no recipient response turns it into an agreed relationship or a total definition of her identity. IDENTITY0036/CHAR0031 retain B0035's uncertain affect labeling, practical network supporting Citrus's home and repaired collaboration with Makoto. This source-local service register supplements that route without a new stable romance, orientation label or readiness promotion. Rivals/limits: Going less often to the maid cafe does not by itself specify a new employment arrangement, quantified work schedule or loss of that activity altogether. The proposed magic and exclusive Ayu are a service idiom and future offer, not demonstrated supernatural effect, unlimited consent or a literal ownership contract. This performance does not settle the attraction object or orientation questions left open by B0035. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0052` / Shirayuki (`arisu-shirayuki`), `402004401:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-041`: A public reading of the pair becomes Shirayuki's account of belonging. She moves from wondering how they appear to accepting a broad family image on the basis of Coney's reported remark. The warmth is hers and Coney's speech is mediated through her report; neither biological kinship nor a complete description of every member's experience follows from the metaphor. IDENTITY0067/CHAR0052 retain one continuous Shirayuki across rural written dialect and learned public standard speech, with later Episode5.0 development not backdated into this earlier child presentation. Coney's CHAR0003 route is maintained, without deriving family consent, a restored later management state or readiness promotion. Rivals/limits: The bystanders' sister label does not establish actual family descent, legal guardianship, exact ages or equal status. The message reports Coney's statement and embarrassment but supplies no own Coney turn or independently accessed interior state. A big-family appraisal does not settle consent, management accountability or the burdens described in other source-local occasions. No new READY or monograph promotion.

- `CHAR-0003` / Coney (`rokusaki-connie-presented`), `402004901:P0-2` / `EV-B0232-042`: The final page makes the status of the preceding care unstable. The reader first receives an apparently direct recognition of overwork and an offer of support, then an explicit theatrical withdrawal in favor of a collective work demand. The sequence permits a reading of concern hidden by teasing and a reading of care actually revoked; the text provides no privileged inner voice to choose between them. The health warning remains something Coney articulated before disavowing it, while neither concern nor labor demand is certified as an implemented practice. IDENTITY0003/CHAR0003 retain the continuous Coney/Nicole literary route and the distinction between earlier agency work and later absence/coda. Prior generativity, imposed direction and boundary costs remain alongside the other Short accounts; no single true-face solution or readiness promotion is added. Rivals/limits: The initial account of late work is Coney's subsequently disavowed statement, not an independently established workload or actual manager location. A familiar teasing convention can make concealed concern plausible, but it does not authorize declaring that the denial is false or that Coney's true feelings have been revealed. Conversely, the explicit all-lies retraction should not be removed to produce an uncomplicated caring-manager portrait; its tonal and ethical disruption remains part of the source. No new READY or monograph promotion.

### B0233 unit-qualified annotations

UNIT0001/Seven Sisters: six legacy forms function as differentiated benchmarks in this route; reported wins over generated images do not replace the historical group's collaboratively produced plurality or prove real-member defeat, renewed organization or legal succession.

UNIT0002/Nanasta: Coney and Player voice new-Nanasta/guts/bonds/own-merits ideals; Player explicitly accepts one reframe and names future work. Those are local statements, not unanimous doctrine, audited technique or completed institutional reform. The one Everyone reply has unresolved membership and anticipates a treat without demonstrating payment or individual assent.
